# Fair Market Rents: Iowa

- Postal code: IA
- State FIPS: 19
- Counties: 99
- Towns: 0

HUD's Fair Market Rents for Iowa, fiscal years 1983 to 2027: 99 counties, each with its rents by bedroom count and the FMR areas it has sat in. 5 of them have changed FMR area at least once (not counting 2006, when HUD renumbered every area).
